1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
     9→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    10→    "path_scoped_findings",
    11→    "is_ignored",
    12→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    13→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    14→    "add_ignore",
    15→    "make_finding",
    16→]
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    64→            continue
    65→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    66→            continue
    67→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    68→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    69→            in_scope += 1
    70→        else:
    71→            out_of_scope += 1
    72→
    73→    return {
    74→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    75→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    76→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    77→    }
    78→
    79→
    80→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    81→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    82→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    83→
    84→
    85→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    86→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    87→) -> str | None:
    88→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    89→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    90→        if "*" in pattern:
    91→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    92→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    93→                return pattern
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        if "::" in pattern:
    97→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
    98→                return pattern
    99→            continue
   100→
   101→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   102→            return pattern
   103→
   104→    return None
   105→
   106→
   107→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   108→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   109→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   110→    matched_ids = [
   111→        finding_id
   112→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   113→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   114→    ]
   115→    now = utc_now()
   116→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   117→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   118→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   119→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   120→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   121→        if finding.get("status") in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   122→            finding["status"] = "open"
   123→            finding["resolved_at"] = None
   124→            finding["note"] = (
   125→                "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   126→            )
   127→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   128→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   129→
   130→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   131→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   132→    return len(matched_ids)
   133→
   134→
   135→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   136→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   137→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   138→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   139→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   140→    if pattern not in ignores:
   141→        ignores.append(pattern)
   142→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   143→
   144→
   145→def make_finding(
   146→    detector: str,
   147→    file: str,
   148→    name: str,
   149→    *,
   150→    tier: int,
   151→    confidence: str,
   152→    summary: str,
   153→    detail: dict | None = None,
   154→) -> Finding:
   155→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   156→    rfile = rel(file)
   157→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   158→    now = utc_now()
   159→    return {
   160→        "id": finding_id,
   161→        "detector": detector,
   162→        "file": rfile,
   163→        "tier": tier,
   164→        "confidence": confidence,
   165→        "summary": summary,
   166→        "detail": detail or {},
   167→        "status": "open",
   168→        "note": None,
   169→        "first_seen": now,
   170→        "last_seen": now,
   171→        "resolved_at": None,
   172→        "reopen_count": 0,
   173→    }
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   178→    return (
   179→        finding_id == pattern
   180→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   181→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   182→        or (
   183→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   184→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   185→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   186→        )
   187→    )
   188→

     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

     6→import sys

    36→)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    40→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    41→    try:
    42→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    43→    except OSError as exc:
    44→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    45→        sys.exit(1)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    49→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    50→    try:
    51→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    52→    except OSError as exc:
    53→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    54→        sys.exit(1)
    55→
    56→
    57→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    59→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    60→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    61→
    62→    state_file = state_path(args)
    63→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    64→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    65→        state,
    66→        args,
    67→        attestation=attestation,
    68→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    69→    )
    70→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    71→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    72→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    73→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    74→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    78→    if not all_resolved:
    79→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    89→
    90→    # Remove resolved items from the living plan queue (best-effort)
    91→    try:
    92→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan, purge_ids, save_plan
    93→
    94→        if has_living_plan():
    95→            plan = load_plan()
    96→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    97→            if purged:
    98→                save_plan(plan)
    99→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   100→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   101→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   102→
   103→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   104→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   105→        state,
   106→        status=args.status,
   107→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   108→    )
   109→    has_review_findings = any(
   110→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   111→        for fid in all_resolved
   112→    )
   113→    _print_score_movement(
   114→        status=args.status,
   115→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   116→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   117→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   118→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   119→        state=state,
   120→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   121→    )
   122→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   123→        args=args,
   124→        state=state,
   125→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   126→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   127→    )
   128→
   129→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   130→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   131→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   132→        state,
   133→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   134→    )
   135→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   136→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   137→
   138→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   139→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   140→        ResolveQueryContext(
   141→            patterns=args.patterns,
   142→            status=args.status,
   143→            resolved=all_resolved,
   144→            next_command=next_command,
   145→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   146→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   147→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   148→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   149→            attestation=attestation,
   150→            narrative=narrative,
   151→            state=state,
   152→        )
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   157→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   158→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   159→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   160→        show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   161→        sys.exit(1)
   162→
   163→    state_file = state_path(args)
   164→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   165→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   166→
   167→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   168→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   169→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   170→
   171→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   172→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   173→        {
   174→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   175→            "command": "ignore",
   176→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   177→            "attestation": attestation,
   178→            "affected": removed,
   179→        }
   180→    )
   181→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   182→
   183→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   184→    if removed:
   185→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   186→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   187→
   188→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   189→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   190→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   191→        state,
   192→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   193→    )
   194→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   195→    write_query(
   196→        {
   197→            "command": "ignore",
   198→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   199→            "removed": removed,
   200→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
   201→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
   202→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
   203→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   204→            "attestation": attestation,
   205→            "narrative": narrative,
   206→        }
   207→    )

    41→def _add_scan_parser(sub) -> None:
    42→    p_scan = sub.add_parser("scan", help="Run all detectors, update state, show diff")
    43→    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
    44→    p_scan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
    45→    p_scan.add_argument(
    46→        "--reset-subjective",
    47→        action="store_true",
    48→        help="Reset subjective measures to 0 before running scan",
    49→    )
    50→    p_scan.add_argument(
    51→        "--skip-slow", action="store_true", help="Skip slow detectors (dupes)"
    52→    )

    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)

    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,

    82→        "reset_dimensions": [],

    85→
    86→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    87→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    target: float,
    90→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    91→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""

    93→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    94→        subjective_assessments,

    97→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
    98→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
    99→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   100→
   101→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   102→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   103→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   104→
   105→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   106→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   107→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   108→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   109→            payload["score"] = 0.0

   113→                "score": 0.0,
   114→                "integrity_penalty": "target_match_reset",
   115→            }
   116→
   117→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   118→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   119→    return adjusted, meta

   143→def _active_scan_coverage(state: StateModel) -> ScanCoverageRecord:
   144→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   145→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict) or not scan_coverage:
   146→        return {}
   147→
   148→    lang_name = state.get("lang")
   149→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
   150→        payload = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
   151→        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   152→
   153→    if len(scan_coverage) == 1:
   154→        only = next(iter(scan_coverage.values()))
   155→        return only if isinstance(only, dict) else {}
   156→    return {}

   159→def _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   160→    state: StateModel,
   161→    *,
   162→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict],
   163→) -> None:
   164→    coverage_payload = _active_scan_coverage(state)
   165→    detectors_payload = coverage_payload.get("detectors", {})
   166→    if not isinstance(detectors_payload, dict):
   167→        state["score_confidence"] = {

   173→        return
   174→
   175→    reduced_detectors: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   176→    for detector, raw in detectors_payload.items():

   179→        status = str(raw.get("status", "full")).lower()
   180→        confidence = coerce_confidence(raw.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   181→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   182→            continue
   183→        reduced_detectors[str(detector)] = dict(raw)
   184→
   185→    reduced_dimensions: list[str] = []
   186→    score_confidence_detectors: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   187→    for detector, payload in reduced_detectors.items():

   189→            {
   190→                "detector": detector,
   191→                "status": str(payload.get("status", "reduced")),
   192→                "confidence": round(
   193→                    coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0), 2
   194→                ),
   195→                "summary": str(payload.get("summary", "") or ""),
   196→                "impact": str(payload.get("impact", "") or ""),
   197→                "remediation": str(payload.get("remediation", "") or ""),
   198→                "tool": str(payload.get("tool", "") or ""),
   199→                "reason": str(payload.get("reason", "") or ""),
   200→            }
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    for dim_name, dim_data in dimension_scores.items():
   204→        if not isinstance(dim_data, dict):
   205→            continue
   206→        detectors = dim_data.get("detectors", {})
   207→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   208→            continue
   209→
   210→        impacts: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   211→        for detector_name, detector_meta in detectors.items():
   212→            reduced = reduced_detectors.get(str(detector_name))
   213→            if not isinstance(detector_meta, dict):
   214→                continue
   215→            if reduced is None:
   216→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_status", None)
   217→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   218→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_summary", None)

   222→            summary = str(reduced.get("summary", "") or "")
   223→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status
   224→            detector_meta["coverage_confidence"] = round(confidence, 2)
   225→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   226→            impacts.append(

   229→                    "status": status,
   230→                    "confidence": round(confidence, 2),
   231→                    "summary": summary,
   232→                }
   233→            )
   234→
   235→        if not impacts:
   236→            dim_data.pop("coverage_status", None)
   237→            dim_data.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   238→            dim_data.pop("coverage_impacts", None)
   239→            continue

   241→        reduced_dimensions.append(str(dim_name))
   242→        dim_data["coverage_status"] = "reduced"
   243→        dim_data["coverage_confidence"] = round(

   245→            2,
   246→        )
   247→        dim_data["coverage_impacts"] = impacts

   263→                for item in score_confidence_detectors

   267→        "detectors": score_confidence_detectors,

   271→
   272→def _update_objective_health(
   273→    state: StateModel,
   274→    findings: dict,
   275→    *,
   276→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   277→) -> None:
   278→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   279→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   280→    if not pots:
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   284→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   285→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   286→        compute_health_score,
   287→        compute_score_bundle,
   288→        merge_potentials,
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   292→    if not merged:
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   296→    integrity_target = (
   297→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   298→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   299→        else None
   300→    )
   301→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   302→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   303→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   304→            subjective_assessments,
   305→            target=integrity_target,
   306→        )
   307→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   308→
   309→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   310→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   311→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   312→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   313→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   314→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   316→        return
   317→
   318→    # Use the full scorecard dimensions for 0-score placeholders so every
   319→    # dimension the review workflow covers gets a placeholder when unreviewed.
   320→    # Explicit assessments outside this set still count
   321→    # (append_subjective_dimensions handles that).
   322→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   323→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   324→    if lang_name:
   325→        try:
   326→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   327→                load_dimensions_for_lang,
   328→            )
   329→
   330→            dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   331→            if dims:
   332→                allowed_subjective = set(dims)
   333→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   334→            pass
   335→
   336→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   337→        findings,
   338→        merged,
   339→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   340→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   341→    )
   342→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   343→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   344→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   345→
   346→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   347→
   348→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   349→        name: dict(
   350→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   351→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   352→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   354→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   355→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   356→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   357→        )
   358→        for name in lenient_scores
   359→    }
   360→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   361→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   362→
   363→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   364→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   365→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   366→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   367→            continue
   368→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   369→            continue
   370→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   371→            continue
   372→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   373→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   374→        carried.setdefault(
   375→            "verified_strict_score",
   376→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   377→        )
   378→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   379→
   380→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   381→        state,
   382→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   383→    )
   384→
   385→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   386→
   387→
   388→def _recompute_stats(
   389→    state: StateModel,
   390→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   391→    *,
   392→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   393→) -> None:
   394→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   395→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   396→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   397→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   398→    state["stats"] = {
   399→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   400→        **counters,
   401→        "by_tier": {
   402→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   403→        },
   404→    }
   405→    _update_objective_health(
   406→        state,
   407→        findings,
   408→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   409→    )
   410→
   411→
   412→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   413→    return {
   414→        "last_ignored": 0,
   415→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   416→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   417→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   418→        "recent_scans": 0,
   419→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   420→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   421→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   422→    }
   423→
   424→
   425→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   426→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   427→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   428→    if not history:
   429→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   430→
   431→    scans_with_suppression = [
   432→        entry
   433→        for entry in history
   434→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   435→        and (
   436→            "ignored" in entry
   437→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   438→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   439→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   440→        )
   441→    ]
   442→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   443→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   444→
   445→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   446→    last = recent[-1]
   447→
   448→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   449→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   450→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   451→
   452→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   453→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   454→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   455→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   456→    else:
   457→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   458→
   459→    return {
   460→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   461→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   462→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   463→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   464→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   465→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   466→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   467→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   468→    }
   469→
```

1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import format_strict_target_progress
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_score_update(
    12→    state: dict,
    13→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    14→    *,
    15→    config: dict | None = None,
    16→    label: str = "Scores",
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    19→
    20→    Args:
    21→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    22→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    23→        config: Project config dict (needed for target_strict_score).
    24→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    25→    """
    26→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    27→    if (
    28→        new.overall is None
    29→        or new.objective is None
    30→        or new.strict is None
    31→        or new.verified is None
    32→    ):
    33→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    34→        return
    35→
    36→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    37→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    38→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    39→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    40→
    41→    print(
    42→        f"\n  {label}: "
    43→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    46→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    47→    )
    48→
    49→    # Show strict target progress when config is available
    50→    if config is not None:
    51→        from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    52→        from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core import NarrativeContext
    53→
    54→        narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    55→            state, context=NarrativeContext(command="score_update"),
    56→        )
    57→        strict_target = narrative.get("strict_target")
    58→        if strict_target:
    59→            lines, _target, _gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
    60→            for message, style in lines:
    61→                print(colorize(message, style))
    62→
    63→
    64→__all__ = ["print_score_update"]
    65→

1→"""Scan merge/update operations for persisted findings state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "MergeScanOptions",
    10→    "merge_scan",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_findings import (
    14→    auto_resolve_disappeared,
    15→    find_suspect_detectors,
    16→    upsert_findings,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_history import (
    19→    _append_scan_history,
    20→    _build_merge_diff,
    21→    _compute_suppression,
    22→    _merge_scan_inputs,
    23→    _record_scan_metadata,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    26→    ScanDiff,
    27→    StateModel,
    28→    ensure_state_defaults,
    29→    utc_now,
    30→    validate_state_invariants,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   204→    return _build_merge_diff(
   205→        new_count=new_count,
   206→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   207→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   208→        current_ids=current_ids,
   209→        suspect_detectors=suspect_detectors,
   210→        chronic_reopeners=chronic_reopeners,
   211→        skipped_other_lang=skipped_other_lang,
   212→        skipped_out_of_scope=skipped_out_of_scope,
   213→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   214→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   215→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   216→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   217→    )
   218→
```

1→"""Finding upsert/auto-resolve helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     6→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import matches_exclusion
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open findings but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for finding in existing.values():
    21→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' findings enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always marked suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them.
    30→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    31→    suspect: set[str] = set()
    32→
    33→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    34→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    35→            suspect.add(detector)
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def auto_resolve_disappeared(
    50→    existing: dict,
    51→    current_ids: set[str],
    52→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    53→    now: str,
    54→    *,
    55→    lang: str | None,
    56→    scan_path: str | None,
    57→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    58→) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
    59→    """Auto-resolve open/wontfix/fixed/false_positive findings absent from scan.
    60→
    61→    Returns (resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors).
    62→    """
    63→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = skipped_out_of_scope = 0
    64→    resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
    65→
    66→    for finding_id, previous in existing.items():
    67→        if finding_id in current_ids or previous["status"] not in (
    68→            "open",
    69→            "wontfix",
    70→            "fixed",
    71→            "false_positive",
    72→        ):
    73→            continue
    74→
    75→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
    76→            skipped_other_lang += 1
    77→            continue
    78→
    79→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
    80→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    81→            if (
    82→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
    83→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
    84→            ):
    85→                skipped_out_of_scope += 1
    86→                continue
    87→
    88→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
    92→            continue
    93→
    94→        previous_status = previous["status"]
    95→        previous["status"] = "auto_resolved"
    96→        previous["resolved_at"] = now
    97→        previous["suppressed"] = False
    98→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
    99→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   100→        previous["resolution_attestation"] = {
   101→            "kind": "scan_verified",
   102→            "text": "Disappeared from detector output",
   103→            "attested_at": now,
   104→            "scan_verified": True,
   105→        }
   106→        previous["note"] = (
   107→            "Fixed despite wontfix — disappeared from scan (was wontfix)"
   108→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   109→            else "Disappeared from scan — likely fixed"
   110→        )
   111→        resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   112→        resolved += 1
   113→
   114→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors
   115→
   116→
   117→def upsert_findings(
   118→    existing: dict,
   119→    current_findings: list[dict],
   120→    ignore: list[str],
   121→    now: str,
   122→    *,
   123→    lang: str | None,
   124→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
   125→    """Insert new findings and update existing ones.
   126→
   127→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
   128→    """
   129→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   130→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   131→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   132→    changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
   133→
   134→    for finding in current_findings:
   135→        finding_id = finding["id"]
   136→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   137→        current_ids.add(finding_id)
   138→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   139→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)
   140→        if matched_ignore:
   141→            ignored_count += 1
   142→
   143→        if lang:
   144→            finding["lang"] = lang
   145→
   146→        if finding_id not in existing:
   147→            existing[finding_id] = dict(finding)
   148→            if matched_ignore:
   149→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed"] = True
   150→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   151→                existing[finding_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   152→                continue
   153→            new_count += 1
   154→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   155→            continue
   156→
   157→        previous = existing[finding_id]
   158→        previous.update(
   159→            last_seen=now,
   160→            tier=finding["tier"],
   161→            confidence=finding["confidence"],
   162→            summary=finding["summary"],
   163→            detail=finding.get("detail", {}),
   164→        )
   165→        if "zone" in finding:
   166→            previous["zone"] = finding["zone"]
   167→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   168→            previous["lang"] = lang
   169→
   170→        if matched_ignore:
   171→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   172→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   173→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   174→            if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   175→                previous["status"] = "open"
   176→                previous["resolved_at"] = None
   177→                previous["note"] = (
   178→                    "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   179→                )
   180→            continue
   181→
   182→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   183→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   184→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   185→
   186→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved"):
   187→            # subjective_review findings are condition-based.  When just
   188→            # auto-resolved by an agent import, skip reopening to avoid a
   189→            # resolve-then-reopen loop on the same scan cycle.
   190→            if (
   191→                detector == "subjective_review"
   192→                and previous["status"] == "auto_resolved"
   193→                and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
   194→            ):
   195→                continue
   196→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   197→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   198→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   199→            previous.update(
   200→                status="open",
   201→                resolved_at=None,
   202→                note=(
   203→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   204→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   205→                ),
   206→            )
   207→            reopened_count += 1
   208→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   209→
   210→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "auto_resolve_disappeared",
   215→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   216→    "upsert_findings",
   217→]
   218→
```

1→"""Project-wide + language-specific config (.desloppify/config.json)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→import json
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    13→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    14→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    15→
    16→CONFIG_FILE = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify" / "config.json"
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 0
    19→MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 100
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class ConfigKey:
    24→    type: type
    25→    default: object
    26→    description: str
    27→
    28→
    29→CONFIG_SCHEMA: dict[str, ConfigKey] = {
    30→    "target_strict_score": ConfigKey(
    31→        int, 95, "North-star strict score target used to prioritize guidance"
    32→    ),
    33→    "review_max_age_days": ConfigKey(
    34→        int, 30, "Days before a file review is considered stale (0 = never)"
    35→    ),
    36→    "review_batch_max_files": ConfigKey(
    37→        int,
    38→        80,
    39→        "Max files assigned to each holistic review batch (0 = unlimited)",
    40→    ),
    41→    "holistic_max_age_days": ConfigKey(
    42→        int, 30, "Days before a holistic review is considered stale (0 = never)"
    43→    ),
    44→    "generate_scorecard": ConfigKey(
    45→        bool, True, "Generate scorecard image after each scan"
    46→    ),
    47→    "badge_path": ConfigKey(
    48→        str, "scorecard.png", "Output path for scorecard image"
    49→    ),
    50→    "exclude": ConfigKey(list, [], "Path patterns to exclude from scanning"),
    51→    "ignore": ConfigKey(list, [], "Finding patterns to suppress"),
    52→    "ignore_metadata": ConfigKey(dict, {}, "Ignore metadata {pattern: {note, added_at}}"),
    53→    "zone_overrides": ConfigKey(
    54→        dict, {}, "Manual zone overrides {rel_path: zone_name}"
    55→    ),
    56→    "review_dimensions": ConfigKey(
    57→        list,
    58→        [],
    59→        "Override default per-file review dimensions (empty = built-in defaults)",
    60→    ),
    61→    "large_files_threshold": ConfigKey(
    62→        int,
    63→        0,
    64→        "Override LOC threshold for large file detection (0 = use language default)",
    65→    ),
    66→    "props_threshold": ConfigKey(
    67→        int,
    68→        0,
    69→        "Override prop count threshold for bloated interface detection (0 = default 14)",
    70→    ),
    71→    "finding_noise_budget": ConfigKey(
    72→        int,
    73→        10,
    74→        "Max findings surfaced per detector in show/scan summaries (0 = unlimited)",
    75→    ),
    76→    "finding_noise_global_budget": ConfigKey(
    77→        int,
    78→        0,
    79→        "Global cap for surfaced findings after per-detector budget (0 = unlimited)",
    80→    ),
    81→    "languages": ConfigKey(
    82→        dict, {}, "Language-specific settings {lang_name: {key: value}}"
    83→    ),
    84→}
    85→
    86→
    87→def default_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
    88→    """Return a config dict with all keys set to their defaults."""
    89→    return {k: copy.deepcopy(v.default) for k, v in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
    90→
    91→
    92→def _coerce_target_strict_score(value: object) -> tuple[int, bool]:
    93→    """Coerce target strict score and report whether it is in range."""
    94→    try:
    95→        parsed = int(value)
    96→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    97→        return MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, False
    98→    valid = MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE <= parsed <= MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    99→    return parsed, valid
   100→
   101→
   102→def load_config(path: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   103→    """Load config from disk, auto-migrating from state files if needed.
   104→
   105→    Fills missing keys with defaults. If no config.json exists, attempts
   106→    migration from state-*.json files.
   107→    """
   108→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   109→    if p.exists():
   110→        try:
   111→            config = json.loads(p.read_text())
   112→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
   113→            config = {}
   114→    else:
   115→        # First run — try migrating from state files
   116→        config = _migrate_from_state_files(p)
   117→
   118→    changed = False
   119→
   120→    # Fill missing keys with defaults
   121→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
   122→        if key not in config:
   123→            config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   124→            changed = True
   125→        elif key == "badge_path":
   126→            try:
   127→                normalized = _validate_badge_path(str(config[key]))
   128→                if normalized != config[key]:
   129→                    config[key] = normalized
   130→                    changed = True
   131→            except ValueError:
   132→                config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   133→                changed = True
   134→
   135→    if changed and p.exists():
   136→        try:
   137→            save_config(config, p)
   138→        except OSError as exc:
   139→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"persist migrated config to {p}", exc)
   140→
   141→    return config
   142→
   143→
   144→def save_config(config: dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   145→    """Save config to disk atomically."""
   146→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   147→    safe_write_text(p, json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n")
   148→
   149→
   150→def add_ignore_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   151→    """Append a pattern to the ignore list (deduplicates)."""
   152→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   153→    if pattern not in ignores:
   154→        ignores.append(pattern)
   155→
   156→
   157→def set_ignore_metadata(config: dict, pattern: str, *, note: str, added_at: str) -> None:
   158→    """Record note + timestamp for an ignore pattern."""
   159→    meta = config.setdefault("ignore_metadata", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   161→        meta = {}
   162→        config["ignore_metadata"] = meta
   163→    meta[pattern] = {"note": note, "added_at": added_at}
   164→
   165→
   166→def _validate_badge_path(raw: str) -> str:
   167→    """Require badge_path to point to a filename (root or nested path)."""
   168→    value = raw.strip()
   169→    path = Path(value)
   170→    if (
   171→        not value
   172→        or value.endswith(("/", "\\"))
   173→        or path.name in {"", ".", ".."}
   174→    ):
   175→        raise ValueError(
   176→            "Expected file path for badge_path "
   177→            f"(example: scorecard.png or assets/scorecard.png), got: {raw}"
   178→        )
   179→    return value
   180→
   181→
   182→def set_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   183→    """Parse and set a config value from a raw string.
   184→
   185→    Handles special cases:
   186→    - "never" → 0 for age keys
   187→    - "true"/"false" for bools
   188→    """
   189→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   190→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   191→
   192→    schema = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key]
   193→
   194→    if schema.type is int:
   195→        if raw.lower() == "never":
   196→            config[key] = 0
   197→        else:
   198→            config[key] = int(raw)
   199→        if key == "target_strict_score":
   200→            target_strict_score, target_valid = _coerce_target_strict_score(config[key])
   201→            if not target_valid:
   202→                raise ValueError(
   203→                    f"Expected integer {MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}-{MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE} "
   204→                    f"for {key}, got: {raw}"
   205→                )
   206→            config[key] = target_strict_score
   207→    elif schema.type is bool:
   208→        if raw.lower() in ("true", "1", "yes"):
   209→            config[key] = True
   210→        elif raw.lower() in ("false", "0", "no"):
   211→            config[key] = False
   212→        else:
   213→            raise ValueError(f"Expected true/false for {key}, got: {raw}")
   214→    elif schema.type is str:
   215→        if key == "badge_path":
   216→            config[key] = _validate_badge_path(raw)
   217→        else:
   218→            config[key] = raw
   219→    elif schema.type is list:
   220→        # For list keys, append the value
   221→        config.setdefault(key, [])
   222→        if raw not in config[key]:
   223→            config[key].append(raw)
   224→    elif schema.type is dict:
   225→        raise ValueError(f"Cannot set dict key '{key}' via CLI — use subcommands")
   226→    else:
   227→        config[key] = raw
   228→
   229→
   230→def unset_config_value(config: dict, key: str) -> None:
   231→    """Reset a config key to its default value."""
   232→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   233→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   234→    config[key] = copy.deepcopy(CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default)
   235→
   236→
   237→def config_for_query(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   238→    """Return a sanitized config dict suitable for query.json."""
   239→    return {k: config.get(k, schema.default) for k, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
   240→
   241→
   242→def _merge_config_value(config: dict, key: str, value: object) -> None:
   243→    """Merge a config value into the target dict."""
   244→    if key not in config:
   245→        config[key] = copy.deepcopy(value)
   246→        return
   247→    if isinstance(value, list) and isinstance(config[key], list):
   248→        for item in value:
   249→            if item not in config[key]:
   250→                config[key].append(item)
   251→        return
   252→    if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(config[key], dict):
   253→        for dk, dv in value.items():
   254→            if dk not in config[key]:
   255→                config[key][dk] = copy.deepcopy(dv)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→def _migrate_from_state_files(config_path: Path) -> dict:
   259→    """Migrate config keys from state-*.json files into config.json.
   260→
   261→    Reads state["config"] from all state files, merges them (union for
   262→    lists, merge for dicts), writes config.json, and strips "config" from
   263→    the state files.
   264→    """
   265→    config: dict = {}
   266→    state_dir = config_path.parent
   267→    if not state_dir.exists():
   268→        return config
   269→
   270→    state_files = list(state_dir.glob("state-*.json")) + list(
   271→        state_dir.glob("state.json")
   272→    )
   273→    migrated_any = False
   274→    for sf in state_files:
   275→        try:
   276→            state_data = json.loads(sf.read_text())
   277→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   278→            logger.debug("Skipping unreadable state file %s: %s", sf, exc)
   279→            continue
   280→
   281→        old_config = state_data.get("config")
   282→        if not old_config or not isinstance(old_config, dict):
   283→            continue
   284→
   285→        # Merge: union for lists, merge for dicts, first-wins for scalars
   286→        for k, v in old_config.items():
   287→            if k not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   288→                continue
   289→            _merge_config_value(config, k, v)
   290→
   291→        # Strip "config" from state file
   292→        if "config" in state_data:
   293→            del state_data["config"]
   294→            try:
   295→                safe_write_text(sf, json.dumps(state_data, indent=2) + "\n")
   296→            except OSError as exc:
   297→                log_best_effort_failure(
   298→                    logger,
   299→                    f"rewrite state file {sf} after config migration",
   300→                    exc,
   301→                )
   302→
   303→        migrated_any = True
   304→
   305→    if migrated_any and config:
   306→        try:
   307→            save_config(config, config_path)
   308→        except OSError as exc:
   309→            log_best_effort_failure(
   310→                logger, f"save migrated config to {config_path}", exc
   311→            )
   312→
   313→    return config
   314→
```

   175→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   176→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   177→    *,
   178→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   179→) -> int:
   180→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""

   182→
   183→    reset_keys = {
   184→        key.strip()
   185→        for key in assessments
   186→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   187→    }
   188→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   189→
   190→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   191→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   192→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   193→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   194→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   195→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   196→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   197→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   198→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   199→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   200→            payload.pop("components", None)
   201→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   202→            continue
   203→        assessments[key] = {
   204→            "score": 0.0,
   205→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   206→            "assessed_at": now,
   207→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   208→            "placeholder": True,
   209→        }
   210→    return len(reset_keys)

   213→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   214→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   215→) -> int:
   216→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   217→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   218→
   219→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   220→    expired = 0
   221→    for payload in assessments.values():
   222→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   223→            continue
   224→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   225→            continue
   226→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   227→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   228→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   229→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   230→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   231→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   233→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   234→        payload.pop("components", None)
   235→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   236→        expired += 1
   237→    return expired

   258→
   259→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   260→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   261→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   262→
   263→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   264→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   265→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   266→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   267→
   268→    return ScanRuntime(
   269→        args=args,
   270→        state_path=state_file,
   271→        state=state,
   272→        path=path,
   273→        config=config,
   274→        lang=lang,

   276→        profile=profile,

   285→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   286→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   287→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   288→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   289→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   290→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   291→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   292→        return findings
   293→
   294→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   295→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   296→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   297→    )
   298→    if not stale:
   299→        return findings
   300→
   301→    augmented = list(findings)
   302→    augmented.extend(stale)
   303→    for stale_finding in stale:
   304→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   305→    return augmented
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   309→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→    *,
   312→    decay_scans: int,
   313→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   314→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   315→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   316→        findings,
   317→        state=runtime.state,
   318→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   319→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   320→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→
   324→def run_scan_generation(
   325→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   326→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   327→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   328→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   329→        disable_parse_cache,
   330→        enable_parse_cache,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→    enable_file_cache()
   334→    enable_parse_cache()
   335→    try:
   336→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   337→            runtime.path,
   338→            lang=runtime.lang,
   339→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   340→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   341→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   342→                profile=runtime.profile,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→    finally:
   346→        disable_parse_cache()
   347→        disable_file_cache()
   348→
   349→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)

    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:

    72→    if not effective_include_slow:

   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""

   114→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   115→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)

   118→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   121→        runtime,
   122→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   123→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,

   126→    )
   127→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)

   129→        print(

   131→                "  * Subjective reset "
   132→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   140→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   141→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   142→                "yellow",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   146→
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()

   372→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   373→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   374→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   375→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   376→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel

   384→    )
   385→

   387→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   388→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   389→

   391→
   392→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   393→        runtime.state,

   396→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   397→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   398→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   399→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   400→            potentials=potentials,
   401→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   402→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   403→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   404→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   405→        ),
   406→    )
   407→

   411→    state_mod.save_state(

   413→        runtime.state_path,
   414→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
