Perfect! Now I have enough information. Let me create a comprehensive summary of the findings.

## Summary: Ignore/Exclude and Scan State Management

Based on my thorough analysis of the desloppify codebase, here's a detailed breakdown of the ignore/exclude and scan state management system:

---

### 1. ALL IGNORE/EXCLUDE ENTRY POINTS

#### CLI Commands for Ignore Patterns:

**a) `ignore` subcommand** — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` (lines 251-267):
- Command: `desloppify ignore <pattern> [--attest "..."] [--state <path>]`
- Handled by: `cmd_ignore_pattern()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py` (lines 156-208)
- UX Flow:
  1. User provides attestation (requires "I have actually" + "not gaming" keywords for anti-gaming)
  2. Pattern is added to `config.ignore[]` via `config_mod.add_ignore_pattern()`
  3. Existing findings matching the pattern are suppressed via `state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)`
  4. State is saved with attestation log entry
  5. Score update is printed with narrative coaching

**b) `resolve` subcommand** — Can resolve findings but does not add ignore patterns directly
- Related but distinct: resolves findings to fixed/wontfix/false_positive/open status
- Handled by: `cmd_resolve()` in same file (lines 57-154)
- UX Flow differs: marks findings with resolution status, can remove from living plan

#### Config Storage:

**Config file location**: `PROJECT_ROOT/.desloppify/config.json`
- **Key**: `"ignore"` — list of patterns (file paths, globs, detector::prefix)
- **Related key**: `"ignore_metadata"` — dict tracking `{pattern: {note, added_at}}`
- Config is loaded in `config.py` with `load_config()` and saved atomically via `safe_write_text()`

#### Exclude Patterns (Different from Ignore):

**Exclude**: `config["exclude"]` — applied during scanning to skip entire files/directories
- Used to filter what the scanner sees (prevents detector runs)
- Unlike ignore, which suppresses already-found findings

---

### 2. STATE MUTATION DURING IGNORE

#### `remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)` — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py` (lines 107-133)

**What it does**:
1. Finds all findings matching the pattern using `is_ignored(finding_id, file, [pattern])`
2. For each matched finding:
   - Sets `finding["suppressed"] = True`
   - Sets `finding["suppressed_at"] = utc_now()` (ISO timestamp)
   - Sets `finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern` (which rule suppressed it)
   - **Critical**: If status is "fixed", "auto_resolved", or "false_positive", reverts status to "open":
     ```python
     if finding.get("status") in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
         finding["status"] = "open"
         finding["resolved_at"] = None
         finding["note"] = "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
     ```
   - This prevents score gaming: you can't mark a finding fixed and then ignore it (it comes back as open)

3. **Calls `_recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))`** to recalculate scores

#### `add_ignore(state, pattern)` — lines 135-143

**Wrapper that**:
1. Appends pattern to `state["config"]["ignore"][]` (deduplicates)
2. Calls `remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)` to suppress matches
3. Returns count of affected findings

#### Finding Matching Logic — `matched_ignore_pattern()` (lines 85-105)

Pattern types supported:
- **Glob patterns** (contain `*`): matched against finding_id if pattern has `::`, else against file path
  - Example: `**/*.test.ts` matches files, `eslint::*` matches finding IDs
- **Detector prefix** (contains `::`): `startswith()` matching on finding_id
  - Example: `eslint::no-unused` suppresses all eslint findings starting with that prefix
- **Exact file path**: matches `file == pattern` or `file == rel(pattern)` (normalized path)
  - Example: `src/index.ts` suppresses all findings in that file

---

### 3. SCORE RECOMPUTATION MECHANISM

#### `_recompute_stats()` — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/scoring.py` (lines 388-410)

**Core logic**:
1. Calls `path_scoped_findings()` to get findings within the current scan scope
2. Calls `_count_findings(findings)` which tallies **all findings by status**:
   - Counts per-status: "open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive"
   - **Includes suppressed findings** — they are still counted in their status!
   - Breaks down by tier (1-4)
3. Sets `state["stats"]` with totals and per-tier breakdown
4. Calls `_update_objective_health()` to compute canonical scores

#### Suppressed Findings Accounting:

**Key point**: Suppressed findings are **counted as open** in score calculations.

The flow in `_recompute_stats` → `_count_findings`:
```python
for finding in findings.values():
    status = finding["status"]  # "open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", etc.
    tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
```

When a finding is suppressed:
- Status is reverted to "open" (if it was "fixed" etc.) to maintain score integrity
- It appears in `stats["open"]` and contributes to score computation
- Score computation in `compute_score_bundle()` (in `scoring.py` module) includes all "open" findings

#### Score Quartet Computation — `_update_objective_health()` (lines 272-386)

Uses dimension scoring from `potentials`:
1. Merges potentials via `merge_potentials()`
2. Applies subjective integrity policy (penalizes clustered subjective scores)
3. Calls `compute_score_bundle()` which:
   - Computes lenient, strict, and verified_strict scores per dimension
   - Aggregates to overall_score, objective_score, strict_score, verified_strict_score
4. All dimensions count open findings equally — no special "suppressed" flag exemption

---

### 4. SCAN RECONCILIATION WITH EXISTING STATE

#### How `merge_scan()` Handles Ignores — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge.py` (lines 108-218)

**Scan merge process**:
1. **Load ignore patterns**: From `options.ignore` parameter or `state["config"]["ignore"]` (line 137)
2. **Call `upsert_findings()`** with the ignore list:
   - New findings from detector output are checked against ignore patterns
   - If matched, new finding is created with `suppressed=True, suppression_pattern=pattern`
   - Ignored findings **do not increment new_count** (line 153-154)
   - Ignored count is tracked separately and reported in merge diff

#### `upsert_findings()` Details — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge_findings.py` (lines 117-210)

For each finding in the current scan:
1. **Check ignore patterns** (line 139): `matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)`
2. **New finding** (lines 146-155):
   - If not in existing state and matched ignore: add with `suppressed=True`, skip new_count
   - If not matched: add with `suppressed=False`, increment new_count, mark detector changed
3. **Existing finding** (lines 157-209):
   - **Always update** tier, confidence, summary, detail, zone, lang
   - If matched ignore:
     - Set `suppressed=True, suppression_pattern=matched_ignore`
     - If status was "fixed/auto_resolved/false_positive": revert to "open" (lines 174-179)
   - If NOT matched (clearing previous suppression):
     - Set `suppressed=False, suppression_pattern=None`
     - If status was "fixed/auto_resolved": reopen it (lines 186-208), increment reopened_count

#### Post-Upsert: Auto-resolution and Stale Marking

After upsert, if a finding disappeared from scan output:
1. **`auto_resolve_disappeared()`** marks findings as "auto_resolved" if not in current scan
2. BUT: Clears suppression flags (lines 97-99) — auto-resolved findings lose suppression
3. **`_mark_stale_on_mechanical_change()`** marks subjective assessments `needs_review_refresh=True` when affected detectors change

#### Final Score Computation

Line 180-184 in merge.py:
```python
_recompute_stats(
    state,
    scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
)
```

This runs AFTER all finding mutations, recalculating stats with current ignore patterns applied.

#### Actual Scan Flow — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py` (lines 392-406)

```python
diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
    runtime.state,
    findings,
    options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
        lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
        scan_path=scan_path_rel,
        force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
        exclude=get_exclusions(),
        potentials=potentials,
        codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
        include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
        ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),  # <-- Ignore patterns passed here
        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
    ),
)
```

Ignore patterns are **loaded from `config.ignore`** every scan and passed to `merge_scan()`.

---

### 5. POST-COMMAND HOOKS & SCORE REPORTING

#### Centralized Score Update Display — `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score_update.py`

**Function**: `print_score_update(state, prev, config=None, label="Scores")`

- Compares new scores (from `state_mod.score_snapshot(state)`) with prev snapshot
- Prints: overall, objective, strict, verified scores with deltas and colors
- If config provided, also shows strict target progress
- Called after every state-changing command (ignore, resolve, fix, review import)

#### Where Score Update is Called:

1. **After `ignore` command** (cmd.py line 186):
   ```python
   print_score_update(state, prev)
   ```

2. **After `resolve` command** (cmd.py line 113-121):
   ```python
   _print_score_movement(
       status=args.status,
       prev_overall=prev.overall,
       prev_objective=prev.objective,
       prev_strict=prev.strict,
       prev_verified=prev.verified,
       state=state,
       has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   )
   ```
   Uses `_print_score_movement()` which calls `print_score_update()` internally

3. **After `scan` command** (scan.py):
   Calls `show_score_delta()` from scan_reporting_summary

#### No Global Dirty/Stale Flag for Ignore Changes

**Critical finding**: There is **NO** `dirty` or `needs_rescan` flag in the state schema.

Instead, the system relies on:
1. **Immediate score recomputation** after ignore command via `_recompute_stats()`
2. **Next scan** always loads `config.ignore` and applies patterns during merge
3. **Subjective assessment staleness** via `needs_review_refresh` flag (only for mechanical finding changes during scan)

---

### 6. THE BUG: Why Ignore Pattern Changes Corrupt Score Accounting

#### The Problem:

When an ignore pattern is added or changed:
1. **State is saved** with findings marked `suppressed=True` and status reverted to "open"
2. **Scores are recomputed** with `_recompute_stats()`, counting suppressed-as-open findings
3. **BUT**: At next scan, `merge_scan()` loads ignore patterns from `config.ignore`
4. If the ignore pattern **changed** (e.g., pattern removed or modified), the matching set of findings changes
5. Findings that were suppressed might now be unsuppressed, or vice versa
6. Their status might still be "open" (from when they were suppressed), creating a mismatch

#### Example Corruption Scenario:

**Initial state**:
- Finding: `eslint::no-unused#src/index.ts` with status "open"
- Ignore pattern added: `eslint::no-*`
- Finding is suppressed, status reverted to "open", score reflects it
- State saved

**Later, ignore pattern changed to `eslint::no-console`**:
- Pattern no longer matches `eslint::no-unused`
- At next scan, `upsert_findings()` sees finding in current output
- Pattern doesn't match, so `suppressed=False`
- **But**: Finding's status is still "open" from suppression reversion
- **Score counts it as open**, but it might have been legitimately fixed

**The mismatch**: Score accounting gets confused because:
- Suppression status (suppressed=True/False) doesn't always correlate with status field
- No "dirty state" marker to trigger full rescoring validation
- No metadata tracking original status before suppression (always reverted to "open")

---

### 7. EXISTING SAFEGUARDS

#### What the System Has:

1. **Suppression metadata**: `suppressed`, `suppressed_at`, `suppression_pattern` fields track why
2. **Status reversion on ignore**: Prevents "mark fixed + ignore" gaming
3. **Immediate recompute**: `_recompute_stats()` called right after ignore command
4. **Scan history**: `scan_history` entries track ignored/raw counts per scan
5. **Subjective staleness**: Mechanical changes mark dimensions `needs_review_refresh=True`
6. **Plan reconciliation**: Living plan is re-reconciled after scan (lines 418-446 in scan_workflow.py)

#### What's Missing:

1. **State invalidation flag**: No "scores are stale due to ignore pattern changes" marker
2. **Suppression history**: No log of when patterns were added/removed/changed
3. **Validation hook**: No check during scan to verify suppressed findings still match their patterns
4. **Score assertion**: No mechanism to validate score consistency before reporting

---

### 8. DETAILED FINDING FIELDS RELATED TO IGNORE

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py`:

```typescript
Finding = {
    id: str                              // "detector::file::name"
    detector: str                        // "eslint", "coverage", etc.
    file: str                            // Relative path
    tier: int                            // 1-4
    confidence: str                      // "high", "low", etc.
    summary: str                         // Brief description
    detail: dict                         // Detector-specific metadata
    status: "open"|"fixed"|"auto_resolved"|"wontfix"|"false_positive"
    note: str | None                     // User/system notes
    first_seen: str                      // ISO timestamp
    last_seen: str                       // ISO timestamp
    resolved_at: str | None              // When marked resolved
    reopen_count: int                    // Times reopened
    
    // Suppression fields (NotRequired)
    suppressed: bool                     // True if matching ignore pattern
    suppressed_at: str | None            // When suppressed
    suppression_pattern: str | None      // Which pattern suppressed it
    
    // Other optional
    resolution_attestation: dict         // Anti-gaming metadata
    lang: str                            // Language code
    zone: str                            // File zone (production/test/config/etc.)
}
```

---

### 9. QUERY OUTPUT TRACKING

Both `ignore` and `resolve` commands write to `query.json`:

**Ignore command** (cmd_ignore_pattern, line 195-207):
```python
write_query({
    "command": "ignore",
    "pattern": args.pattern,
    "removed": removed,
    "overall_score": scores.overall,
    "objective_score": scores.objective,
    "strict_score": scores.strict,
    "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    "attestation": attestation,
    "narrative": narrative,
})
```

This provides historical record of ignore operations but not of pattern changes over time.

---

### SUMMARY TABLE

| Aspect | Details |
|--------|---------|
| **Ignore entry points** | `desloppify ignore <pattern>` CLI command |
| **Config storage** | `config.json` with `ignore: [list]` and `ignore_metadata: {pattern: {note, added_at}}` |
| **State mutation** | `remove_ignored_findings()` → sets `suppressed=True`, reverts status to "open", calls `_recompute_stats()` |
| **Score accounting** | Suppressed findings counted as "open" in all score calculations |
| **Scan reconciliation** | `merge_scan()` loads patterns from `config.ignore` and applies via `upsert_findings()` |
| **Post-command reporting** | `print_score_update()` displays quartet with deltas after ignore/resolve |
| **Dirty flag mechanism** | NONE — relies on immediate recompute + next scan application |
| **Stale marking** | Only `needs_review_refresh` for mechanical detector changes during scan |
| **Bug risk** | Pattern change between saves can cause status/suppression mismatches without detection |

> DEVELOPER

Design an implementation plan for fixing the ignore/exclude score corruption bug in desloppify (GitHub issue #168).

## Problem

When `desloppify ignore "pattern"` is run:
1. `remove_ignored_findings()` in `engine/_state/filtering.py:107-132` marks matched findings as `suppressed=True`
2. BUT it also reopens resolved findings (`fixed/auto_resolved/false_positive` → `open`) at line 121-126
3. `_recompute_stats()` in `engine/_state/scoring.py:388-409` calls `_count_findings()` which does NOT filter suppressed findings — they all count
4. Score computation via `_iter_scoring_candidates()` in `engine/_scoring/detection.py:35-46` also does NOT filter suppressed findings
5. Result: score collapses because reopened+suppressed findings count as open debt
6. A normal rescan doesn't recover because the state has corrupted status values

## Current Architecture

### Ignore vs Exclude
- **Ignore** (`config.ignore`): Suppresses findings after detection. Has CLI command `desloppify ignore <pattern> --attest "..."`. Managed in `core/config.py:add_ignore_pattern()` and `engine/_state/filtering.py:remove_ignored_findings()`.
- **Exclude** (`config.exclude`): Path patterns to skip during scanning. Applied at startup via `cli.py:_apply_persisted_exclusions()`. NO dedicated CLI command — only `--exclude` flag on main parser and manual config editing.

### Scoring Pipeline
- `_recompute_stats()` → `_count_findings(findings)` — tallies all findings by status (no suppression filter)
- `_update_objective_health()` → `compute_score_bundle()` → `compute_dimension_scores_by_mode()` → `detector_stats_by_mode()` → `_iter_scoring_candidates()` — iterates findings filtered by detector and zone only (no suppression filter)
- Both file-based and finding-based scoring paths go through `_iter_scoring_candidates()`

### State Schema
Finding fields relevant to suppression:
- `suppressed: bool` — True if matching ignore pattern
- `suppressed_at: str | None` — When suppressed
- `suppression_pattern: str | None` — Which pattern matched
- `status: str` — "open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive"

## User Requirements
1. Fix the score corruption bug — suppressed findings must not affect scoring
2. Add an `exclude` CLI command (currently no subcommand for adding exclude patterns)
3. After ignore/exclude config changes, prompt user to rescan (flag state as needing rebaseline)
4. Every command should warn when state needs rescan

## Design Constraints
- The anti-gaming attestation requirement already exists and is sufficient — don't need the reopen-to-open hack
- Keep changes minimal and focused
- Don't alter scoring formulas
- Existing tests must pass

## Key Files
- `desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py` — `remove_ignored_findings()`, `add_ignore()`
- `desloppify/engine/_state/scoring.py` — `_count_findings()`, `_recompute_stats()`
- `desloppify/engine/_scoring/detection.py` — `_iter_scoring_candidates()`, `detector_stats_by_mode()`, `_file_based_failures_by_mode()`
- `desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py` — `cmd_ignore_pattern()`
- `desloppify/core/config.py` — `add_ignore_pattern()`, `CONFIG_SCHEMA`
- `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` — CLI command registration
- `desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score_update.py` — post-command score display
- `desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py` — state schema

Please design a concrete, step-by-step implementation plan covering:
1. The scoring fix (making suppressed findings invisible to scoring)
2. Removing the problematic reopen-to-open behavior
3. Adding `needs_rescan` state flag
4. Adding `exclude` CLI subcommand
5. Warning on all commands when needs_rescan is set
6. Test strategy

```
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":

   108→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""

   110→    matched_ids = [
   111→        finding_id
   112→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   113→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])

   118→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   119→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now

   126→            )

   128→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   129→
   130→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   131→    validate_state_invariants(state)

   136→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   137→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   138→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   139→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   140→    if pattern not in ignores:

   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   178→    return (
   179→        finding_id == pattern
   180→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   181→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   182→        or (
   183→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   184→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   185→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   186→        )
   187→    )
```

```
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    16→
    17→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = ("open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive")
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,
    81→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    82→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    83→    }
    84→
    85→
    86→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    87→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    target: float,
    90→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    91→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    92→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    93→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    94→        subjective_assessments,
    95→        target=normalized_target,
    96→    )
    97→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
    98→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
    99→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   100→
   101→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   102→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   103→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   104→
   105→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   106→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   107→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   108→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   109→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   110→            payload["integrity_penalty"] = "target_match_reset"
   111→        else:
   112→            adjusted[dimension] = {
   113→                "score": 0.0,
   114→                "integrity_penalty": "target_match_reset",
   115→            }
   116→
   117→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   118→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   119→    return adjusted, meta
   120→
   121→
   122→def _aggregate_scores(
   123→    dim_scores: dict, compute_health_score_fn
   124→) -> dict[str, float]:
   125→    """Derive the 4 aggregate scores from dimension-level data."""
   126→    mechanical = {
   127→        n: d
   128→        for n, d in dim_scores.items()
   129→        if "subjective_assessment" not in d.get("detectors", {})
   130→    }
   131→    return {
   132→        "overall_score": compute_health_score_fn(dim_scores),
   133→        "strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   134→            dim_scores, score_key="strict_score"
   135→        ),
   136→        "objective_score": compute_health_score_fn(mechanical),
   137→        "verified_strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   138→            mechanical, [REDACTED]
   139→        ),
   140→    }
   141→
   142→
   143→def _active_scan_coverage(state: StateModel) -> ScanCoverageRecord:
   144→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   145→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict) or not scan_coverage:
   146→        return {}
   147→
   148→    lang_name = state.get("lang")
   149→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
   150→        payload = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
   151→        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   152→
   153→    if len(scan_coverage) == 1:
   154→        only = next(iter(scan_coverage.values()))
   155→        return only if isinstance(only, dict) else {}
   156→    return {}
   157→
   158→
   159→def _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   160→    state: StateModel,
   161→    *,
   162→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict],
   163→) -> None:
   164→    coverage_payload = _active_scan_coverage(state)
   165→    detectors_payload = coverage_payload.get("detectors", {})
   166→    if not isinstance(detectors_payload, dict):
   167→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   168→            "status": "full",
   169→            "confidence": 1.0,
   170→            "detectors": [],
   171→            "dimensions": [],
   172→        }
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    reduced_detectors: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   176→    for detector, raw in detectors_payload.items():
   177→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
   178→            continue
   179→        status = str(raw.get("status", "full")).lower()
   180→        confidence = coerce_confidence(raw.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   181→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   182→            continue
   183→        reduced_detectors[str(detector)] = dict(raw)
   184→
   185→    reduced_dimensions: list[str] = []
   186→    score_confidence_detectors: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   187→    for detector, payload in reduced_detectors.items():
   188→        score_confidence_detectors.append(
   189→            {
   190→                "detector": detector,
   191→                "status": str(payload.get("status", "reduced")),
   192→                "confidence": round(
   193→                    coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0), 2
   194→                ),
   195→                "summary": str(payload.get("summary", "") or ""),
   196→                "impact": str(payload.get("impact", "") or ""),
   197→                "remediation": str(payload.get("remediation", "") or ""),
   198→                "tool": str(payload.get("tool", "") or ""),
   199→                "reason": str(payload.get("reason", "") or ""),
   200→            }
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    for dim_name, dim_data in dimension_scores.items():
   204→        if not isinstance(dim_data, dict):
   205→            continue
   206→        detectors = dim_data.get("detectors", {})
   207→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   208→            continue
   209→
   210→        impacts: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   211→        for detector_name, detector_meta in detectors.items():
   212→            reduced = reduced_detectors.get(str(detector_name))
   213→            if not isinstance(detector_meta, dict):
   214→                continue
   215→            if reduced is None:
   216→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_status", None)
   217→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   218→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_summary", None)
   219→                continue
   220→            confidence = coerce_confidence(reduced.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   221→            status = str(reduced.get("status", "reduced"))
   222→            summary = str(reduced.get("summary", "") or "")
   223→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status
   224→            detector_meta["coverage_confidence"] = round(confidence, 2)
   225→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   226→            impacts.append(
   227→                {
   228→                    "detector": str(detector_name),
   229→                    "status": status,
   230→                    "confidence": round(confidence, 2),
   231→                    "summary": summary,
   232→                }
   233→            )
   234→
   235→        if not impacts:
   236→            dim_data.pop("coverage_status", None)
   237→            dim_data.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   238→            dim_data.pop("coverage_impacts", None)
   239→            continue
   240→
   241→        reduced_dimensions.append(str(dim_name))
   242→        dim_data["coverage_status"] = "reduced"
   243→        dim_data["coverage_confidence"] = round(
   244→            min(coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0) for item in impacts),
   245→            2,
   246→        )
   247→        dim_data["coverage_impacts"] = impacts
   248→
   249→    if not score_confidence_detectors:
   250→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   251→            "status": "full",
   252→            "confidence": 1.0,
   253→            "detectors": [],
   254→            "dimensions": [],
   255→        }
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    state["score_confidence"] = {
   259→        "status": "reduced",
   260→        "confidence": round(
   261→            min(
   262→                coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   263→                for item in score_confidence_detectors
   264→            ),
   265→            2,
   266→        ),
   267→        "detectors": score_confidence_detectors,
   268→        "dimensions": sorted(set(reduced_dimensions)),
   269→    }
   270→
   271→
   272→def _update_objective_health(
   273→    state: StateModel,
   274→    findings: dict,
   275→    *,
   276→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   277→) -> None:
   278→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   279→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   280→    if not pots:
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   284→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   285→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   286→        compute_health_score,
   287→        compute_score_bundle,
   288→        merge_potentials,
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   292→    if not merged:
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   296→    integrity_target = (
   297→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   298→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   299→        else None
   300→    )
   301→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   302→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   303→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   304→            subjective_assessments,
   305→            target=integrity_target,
   306→        )
   307→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   308→
   309→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   310→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   311→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   312→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   313→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   314→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   316→        return
   317→
   318→    # Use the full scorecard dimensions for 0-score placeholders so every
   319→    # dimension the review workflow covers gets a placeholder when unreviewed.
   320→    # Explicit assessments outside this set still count
   321→    # (append_subjective_dimensions handles that).
   322→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   323→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   324→    if lang_name:
   325→        try:
   326→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   327→                load_dimensions_for_lang,
   328→            )
   329→
   330→            dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   331→            if dims:
   332→                allowed_subjective = set(dims)
   333→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   334→            pass
   335→
   336→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   337→        findings,
   338→        merged,
   339→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   340→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   341→    )
   342→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   343→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   344→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   345→
   346→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   347→
   348→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   349→        name: dict(
   350→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   351→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   352→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   354→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   355→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   356→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   357→        )
   358→        for name in lenient_scores
   359→    }
   360→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   361→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   362→
   363→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   364→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   365→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   366→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   367→            continue
   368→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   369→            continue
   370→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   371→            continue
   372→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   373→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   374→        carried.setdefault(
   375→            "verified_strict_score",
   376→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   377→        )
   378→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   379→
   380→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   381→        state,
   382→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   383→    )
   384→
   385→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   386→
   387→
   388→def _recompute_stats(
   389→    state: StateModel,
   390→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   391→    *,
   392→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   393→) -> None:
   394→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   395→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   396→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   397→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   398→    state["stats"] = {
   399→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   400→        **counters,
   401→        "by_tier": {
   402→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   403→        },
   404→    }
   405→    _update_objective_health(
   406→        state,
   407→        findings,
   408→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   409→    )
   410→
   411→
   412→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   413→    return {
   414→        "last_ignored": 0,
   415→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   416→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   417→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   418→        "recent_scans": 0,
   419→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   420→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   421→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   422→    }
   423→
   424→
   425→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   426→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   427→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   428→    if not history:
   429→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   430→
   431→    scans_with_suppression = [
   432→        entry
   433→        for entry in history
   434→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   435→        and (
   436→            "ignored" in entry
   437→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   438→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   439→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   440→        )
   441→    ]
   442→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   443→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   444→
   445→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   446→    last = recent[-1]
   447→
   448→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   449→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   450→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   451→
   452→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   453→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   454→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   455→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   456→    else:
   457→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   458→
   459→    return {
   460→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   461→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   462→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   463→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   464→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   465→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   466→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   467→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   468→    }
   469→
```

```
1→"""Per-detector scoring calculations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     8→    CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS,
     9→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    10→    HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→    detector_policy,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Finding
    16→
    17→# Tiered file-count cap thresholds for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    18→# Controls how many findings per file contribute to the weighted failure sum.
    19→_FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD = 6     # findings in file for high cap
    20→_FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD = 3      # findings in file for mid cap
    21→_FILE_CAP_HIGH = 2.0             # cap value at high concentration
    22→_FILE_CAP_MID = 1.5              # cap value at mid concentration
    23→_FILE_CAP_LOW = 1.0              # cap value at low concentration (1-2 findings)
    24→
    25→
    26→def merge_potentials(potentials_by_lang: dict[str, dict[str, int]]) -> dict[str, int]:
    27→    """Sum potentials across languages per detector."""
    28→    merged: dict[str, int] = {}
    29→    for lang_potentials in potentials_by_lang.values():
    30→        for detector, count in lang_potentials.items():
    31→            merged[detector] = merged.get(detector, 0) + count
    32→    return merged
    33→
    34→
    35→def _iter_scoring_candidates(
    36→    detector: str,
    37→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    38→    excluded_zones: frozenset[str],
    39→):
    40→    """Yield in-scope findings for a detector (zone-filtered)."""
    41→    for finding in findings.values():
    42→        if finding.get("detector") != detector:
    43→            continue
    44→        if finding.get("zone", "production") in excluded_zones:
    45→            continue
    46→        yield finding
    47→
    48→
    49→def _finding_weight(finding: Finding, *, use_loc_weight: bool) -> float:
    50→    """Compute the scoring weight for a single finding."""
    51→    if use_loc_weight:
    52→        return finding.get("detail", {}).get("loc_weight", 1.0)
    53→    return CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(finding.get("confidence", "medium"), 0.7)
    54→
    55→
    56→def _file_count_cap(findings_in_file: int) -> float:
    57→    """Tiered cap for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    58→
    59→    Keeps file-count denominator semantics while preserving concentration signal:
    60→    1-2 findings => _FILE_CAP_LOW, 3-5 => _FILE_CAP_MID, 6+ => _FILE_CAP_HIGH.
    61→    """
    62→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD:
    63→        return _FILE_CAP_HIGH
    64→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD:
    65→        return _FILE_CAP_MID
    66→    return _FILE_CAP_LOW
    67→
    68→
    69→@dataclass
    70→class _ModeAccum:
    71→    """Per-mode accumulator for file-based detector scoring."""
    72→
    73→    by_file: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    74→    by_file_count: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    75→    file_cap: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    76→    holistic_sum: float = 0.0
    77→    issue_count: int = 0
    78→
    79→
    80→def _file_based_failures_by_mode(
    81→    detector: str,
    82→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    83→    policy,
    84→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]]:
    85→    """Accumulate weighted failures by score mode for file-based detectors."""
    86→    accum: dict[ScoreMode, _ModeAccum] = {mode: _ModeAccum() for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    87→
    88→    for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones):
    89→        status = finding.get("status", "open")
    90→        holistic = finding.get("file") == "." and finding.get("detail", {}).get(
    91→            "holistic"
    92→        )
    93→
    94→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    95→            if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
    96→                continue
    97→
    98→            if holistic:
    99→                accum[mode].holistic_sum += (
   100→                    _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False) * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
   101→                )
   102→                accum[mode].issue_count += 1
   103→                continue
   104→
   105→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=policy.use_loc_weight)
   106→            file_key = finding.get("file", "")
   107→            a = accum[mode]
   108→            a.by_file[file_key] = a.by_file.get(file_key, 0.0) + weight
   109→            a.by_file_count[file_key] = a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0) + 1
   110→            if policy.use_loc_weight and file_key not in a.file_cap:
   111→                a.file_cap[file_key] = weight
   112→            a.issue_count += 1
   113→
   114→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]] = {}
   115→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   116→        a = accum[mode]
   117→        if policy.use_loc_weight:
   118→            weighted = sum(
   119→                min(weighted_sum, a.file_cap.get(file_key, weighted_sum))
   120→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   121→            )
   122→        else:
   123→            weighted = sum(
   124→                min(weighted_sum, _file_count_cap(a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0)))
   125→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   126→            )
   127→        out[mode] = (a.issue_count, weighted + a.holistic_sum)
   128→    return out
   129→
   130→
   131→def detector_stats_by_mode(
   132→    detector: str,
   133→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   134→    potential: int,
   135→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]]:
   136→    """Compute (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures) for each score mode."""
   137→    if potential <= 0:
   138→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   139→
   140→    # Review and concern findings are scored via subjective assessments only —
   141→    # exclude them from the detection-side scoring pipeline so resolving these
   142→    # findings never changes the score directly.
   143→    if detector in ("review", "concerns"):
   144→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   145→
   146→    policy = detector_policy(detector)
   147→
   148→    if policy.file_based:
   149→        mode_failures = _file_based_failures_by_mode(detector, findings, policy)
   150→    else:
   151→        issue_count: dict[ScoreMode, int] = {mode: 0 for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   152→        weighted_failures: dict[ScoreMode, float] = {
   153→            mode: 0.0 for mode in SCORING_MODES
   154→        }
   155→
   156→        for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(
   157→            detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones
   158→        ):
   159→            status = finding.get("status", "open")
   160→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False)
   161→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   162→                if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
   163→                    continue
   164→                issue_count[mode] += 1
   165→                weighted_failures[mode] += weight
   166→
   167→        mode_failures = {
   168→            mode: (issue_count[mode], weighted_failures[mode]) for mode in SCORING_MODES
   169→        }
   170→
   171→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]] = {}
   172→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   173→        issues, weighted = mode_failures[mode]
   174→        pass_rate = max(0.0, (potential - weighted) / potential)
   175→        out[mode] = (pass_rate, issues, weighted)
   176→    return out
   177→
   178→
   179→def detector_pass_rate(
   180→    detector: str,
   181→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   182→    potential: int,
   183→    *,
   184→    strict: bool = False,
   185→) -> tuple[float, int, float]:
   186→    """Pass rate for one detector.
   187→
   188→    Returns (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures).
   189→    Zero potential -> (1.0, 0, 0.0).
   190→    """
   191→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   192→    return detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)[mode]
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = [
   196→    "detector_pass_rate",
   197→    "detector_stats_by_mode",
   198→    "merge_potentials",
   199→]
   200→
```

```
    57→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    59→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    60→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    61→
    62→    state_file = state_path(args)

    66→        args,
    67→        attestation=attestation,
    68→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    69→    )
    70→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    71→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    72→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    73→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    74→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    78→    if not all_resolved:
    79→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    83→                "yellow",

    91→    try:

   102→

   104→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(

   109→    has_review_findings = any(

   111→        for fid in all_resolved

   135→    if narrative.get("milestone"):

   139→    _write_resolve_query_entry(

   156→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
```

```
1→"""Project-wide + language-specific config (.desloppify/config.json)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→import json
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    13→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    14→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    15→
    16→CONFIG_FILE = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify" / "config.json"
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 0
    19→MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 100
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class ConfigKey:
    24→    type: type
    25→    default: object
    26→    description: str

    49→    ),
    50→    "exclude": ConfigKey(list, [], "Path patterns to exclude from scanning"),
    51→    "ignore": ConfigKey(list, [], "Finding patterns to suppress"),

    53→    "zone_overrides": ConfigKey(

    55→    ),

    58→        [],
    59→        "Override default per-file review dimensions (empty = built-in defaults)",
    60→    ),
    61→    "large_files_threshold": ConfigKey(
    62→        int,
    63→        0,
    64→        "Override LOC threshold for large file detection (0 = use language default)",
    65→    ),
    66→    "props_threshold": ConfigKey(
    67→        int,
    68→        0,
    69→        "Override prop count threshold for bloated interface detection (0 = default 14)",
    70→    ),

    72→        int,
    73→        10,
    74→        "Max findings surfaced per detector in show/scan summaries (0 = unlimited)",
    75→    ),
    76→    "finding_noise_global_budget": ConfigKey(
    77→        int,
    78→        0,
    79→        "Global cap for surfaced findings after per-detector budget (0 = unlimited)",

    83→    ),

    85→
    86→
    87→def default_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
    88→    """Return a config dict with all keys set to their defaults."""
    89→    return {k: copy.deepcopy(v.default) for k, v in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
    90→
    91→
    92→def _coerce_target_strict_score(value: object) -> tuple[int, bool]:
    93→    """Coerce target strict score and report whether it is in range."""
    94→    try:
    95→        parsed = int(value)
    96→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    97→        return MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, False
    98→    valid = MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE <= parsed <= MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    99→    return parsed, valid
   100→
   101→
   102→def load_config(path: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   103→    """Load config from disk, auto-migrating from state files if needed.
   104→
   105→    Fills missing keys with defaults. If no config.json exists, attempts
   106→    migration from state-*.json files.
   107→    """
   108→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   109→    if p.exists():
   110→        try:
   111→            config = json.loads(p.read_text())
   112→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
   113→            config = {}
   114→    else:
   115→        # First run — try migrating from state files
   116→        config = _migrate_from_state_files(p)
   117→
   118→    changed = False
   119→
   120→    # Fill missing keys with defaults
   121→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
   122→        if key not in config:
   123→            config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   124→            changed = True
   125→        elif key == "badge_path":
   126→            try:
   127→                normalized = _validate_badge_path(str(config[key]))
   128→                if normalized != config[key]:
   129→                    config[key] = normalized
   130→                    changed = True
   131→            except ValueError:
   132→                config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   133→                changed = True
   134→
   135→    if changed and p.exists():
   136→        try:
   137→            save_config(config, p)
   138→        except OSError as exc:
   139→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"persist migrated config to {p}", exc)
   140→
   141→    return config
   142→
   143→
   144→def save_config(config: dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   145→    """Save config to disk atomically."""
   146→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   147→    safe_write_text(p, json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n")
   148→
   149→
   150→def add_ignore_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   151→    """Append a pattern to the ignore list (deduplicates)."""
   152→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   153→    if pattern not in ignores:
   154→        ignores.append(pattern)
   155→
   156→
   157→def set_ignore_metadata(config: dict, pattern: str, *, note: str, added_at: str) -> None:
   158→    """Record note + timestamp for an ignore pattern."""
   159→    meta = config.setdefault("ignore_metadata", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   161→        meta = {}
   162→        config["ignore_metadata"] = meta
   163→    meta[pattern] = {"note": note, "added_at": added_at}
   164→
   165→
   166→def _validate_badge_path(raw: str) -> str:
   167→    """Require badge_path to point to a filename (root or nested path)."""
   168→    value = raw.strip()
   169→    path = Path(value)
   170→    if (
   171→        not value
   172→        or value.endswith(("/", "\\"))
   173→        or path.name in {"", ".", ".."}
   174→    ):
   175→        raise ValueError(
   176→            "Expected file path for badge_path "
   177→            f"(example: scorecard.png or assets/scorecard.png), got: {raw}"
   178→        )
   179→    return value
   180→
   181→
   182→def set_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   183→    """Parse and set a config value from a raw string.
   184→
   185→    Handles special cases:
   186→    - "never" → 0 for age keys
   187→    - "true"/"false" for bools
   188→    """
   189→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   190→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   191→
   192→    schema = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key]
   193→
   194→    if schema.type is int:
   195→        if raw.lower() == "never":
   196→            config[key] = 0
   197→        else:
   198→            config[key] = int(raw)
   199→        if key == "target_strict_score":
   200→            target_strict_score, target_valid = _coerce_target_strict_score(config[key])
   201→            if not target_valid:
   202→                raise ValueError(
   203→                    f"Expected integer {MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}-{MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE} "
   204→                    f"for {key}, got: {raw}"
   205→                )
   206→            config[key] = target_strict_score
   207→    elif schema.type is bool:
   208→        if raw.lower() in ("true", "1", "yes"):
   209→            config[key] = True
   210→        elif raw.lower() in ("false", "0", "no"):
   211→            config[key] = False
   212→        else:
   213→            raise ValueError(f"Expected true/false for {key}, got: {raw}")
   214→    elif schema.type is str:
   215→        if key == "badge_path":
   216→            config[key] = _validate_badge_path(raw)
   217→        else:
   218→            config[key] = raw
   219→    elif schema.type is list:
   220→        # For list keys, append the value
   221→        config.setdefault(key, [])
   222→        if raw not in config[key]:
   223→            config[key].append(raw)
   224→    elif schema.type is dict:
   225→        raise ValueError(f"Cannot set dict key '{key}' via CLI — use subcommands")
   226→    else:
   227→        config[key] = raw
   228→
   229→
   230→def unset_config_value(config: dict, key: str) -> None:
   231→    """Reset a config key to its default value."""
   232→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   233→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   234→    config[key] = copy.deepcopy(CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default)
   235→
   236→
   237→def config_for_query(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   238→    """Return a sanitized config dict suitable for query.json."""
   239→    return {k: config.get(k, schema.default) for k, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
   240→
   241→
   242→def _merge_config_value(config: dict, key: str, value: object) -> None:
   243→    """Merge a config value into the target dict."""
   244→    if key not in config:
   245→        config[key] = copy.deepcopy(value)
   246→        return
   247→    if isinstance(value, list) and isinstance(config[key], list):
   248→        for item in value:
   249→            if item not in config[key]:
   250→                config[key].append(item)
   251→        return
   252→    if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(config[key], dict):
   253→        for dk, dv in value.items():
   254→            if dk not in config[key]:
   255→                config[key][dk] = copy.deepcopy(dv)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→def _migrate_from_state_files(config_path: Path) -> dict:
   259→    """Migrate config keys from state-*.json files into config.json.
   260→
   261→    Reads state["config"] from all state files, merges them (union for
   262→    lists, merge for dicts), writes config.json, and strips "config" from
   263→    the state files.
   264→    """
   265→    config: dict = {}
   266→    state_dir = config_path.parent
   267→    if not state_dir.exists():
   268→        return config
   269→
   270→    state_files = list(state_dir.glob("state-*.json")) + list(
   271→        state_dir.glob("state.json")
   272→    )
   273→    migrated_any = False
   274→    for sf in state_files:
   275→        try:
   276→            state_data = json.loads(sf.read_text())
   277→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   278→            logger.debug("Skipping unreadable state file %s: %s", sf, exc)
   279→            continue
   280→
   281→        old_config = state_data.get("config")
   282→        if not old_config or not isinstance(old_config, dict):
   283→            continue
   284→
   285→        # Merge: union for lists, merge for dicts, first-wins for scalars
   286→        for k, v in old_config.items():
   287→            if k not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   288→                continue
   289→            _merge_config_value(config, k, v)
   290→
   291→        # Strip "config" from state file
   292→        if "config" in state_data:
   293→            del state_data["config"]
   294→            try:
   295→                safe_write_text(sf, json.dumps(state_data, indent=2) + "\n")
   296→            except OSError as exc:
   297→                log_best_effort_failure(
   298→                    logger,
   299→                    f"rewrite state file {sf} after config migration",
   300→                    exc,
   301→                )
   302→
   303→        migrated_any = True
   304→
   305→    if migrated_any and config:
   306→        try:
   307→            save_config(config, config_path)
   308→        except OSError as exc:
   309→            log_best_effort_failure(
   310→                logger, f"save migrated config to {config_path}", exc
   311→            )
   312→
   313→    return config
   314→
```

```
   181→STATE_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
   182→STATE_FILE = STATE_DIR / "state.json"
   183→CURRENT_VERSION = 1
   184→
   185→
   186→def utc_now() -> str:
   187→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   188→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   189→
   190→
   191→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   192→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   193→    return {
   194→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   195→        "created": utc_now(),
   196→        "last_scan": None,
   197→        "scan_count": 0,
   198→        "overall_score": 0,
   199→        "objective_score": 0,
   200→        "strict_score": 0,
   201→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   202→        "stats": {},
   203→        "findings": {},
   204→        "scan_coverage": {},
   205→        "score_confidence": {},
   206→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   207→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   208→    }
   209→
   210→
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}
   228→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   229→        state["scan_history"] = []
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   231→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   233→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   235→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   236→
   237→    findings = state["findings"]
   238→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   239→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   240→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   241→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   245→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   246→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   247→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   248→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   249→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   250→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   251→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   252→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   253→            finding.get("status"),
   254→            default="open",
   255→        )
   256→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   257→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   258→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   259→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   260→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   261→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   262→        )
   263→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   264→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   265→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   266→
   267→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   268→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   269→
   270→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   271→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   272→            continue
   273→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   274→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   275→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   276→
   277→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   278→    return None
   279→
   280→
   281→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   282→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   283→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   284→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   285→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   286→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   287→
   288→    findings = state["findings"]
   289→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   290→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   291→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   292→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   293→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   294→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   295→            raise ValueError(
   296→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   297→            )
   298→
   299→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   300→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   301→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   302→
   303→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   304→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   305→            raise ValueError(
   306→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   307→            )
   308→
   309→
   310→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
   311→    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
   312→    if isinstance(obj, set):
   313→        return sorted(obj)
   314→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
   315→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
   316→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
   317→        return obj.isoformat()
   318→    raise TypeError(
   319→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   324→    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   325→    return state.get("overall_score")
   326→
   327→
   328→def get_objective_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   329→    """Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)."""
   330→    return state.get("objective_score")
   331→
   332→
   333→def get_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   334→    """Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   335→    return state.get("strict_score")
   336→
   337→
   338→def get_verified_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   339→    """Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes.
   340→
   341→    Returns None if no scan-verified score exists yet (fresh state or
   342→    no scan has run). Does not fall back to the unverified strict_score.
   343→    """
   344→    return state.get("verified_strict_score")
   345→
```

```
1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import format_strict_target_progress
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_score_update(
    12→    state: dict,
    13→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    14→    *,
    15→    config: dict | None = None,
    16→    label: str = "Scores",
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    19→
    20→    Args:
    21→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    22→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    23→        config: Project config dict (needed for target_strict_score).
    24→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    25→    """
    26→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    27→    if (
    28→        new.overall is None
    29→        or new.objective is None
    30→        or new.strict is None
    31→        or new.verified is None
    32→    ):
    33→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    34→        return
    35→
    36→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    37→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    38→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    39→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    40→
    41→    print(
    42→        f"\n  {label}: "
    43→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    46→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    47→    )
    48→
    49→    # Show strict target progress when config is available
    50→    if config is not None:
    51→        from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    52→        from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core import NarrativeContext
    53→
    54→        narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    55→            state, context=NarrativeContext(command="score_update"),
    56→        )
    57→        strict_target = narrative.get("strict_target")
    58→        if strict_target:
    59→            lines, _target, _gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
    60→            for message, style in lines:
    61→                print(colorize(message, style))
    62→
    63→
    64→__all__ = ["print_score_update"]
    65→
```

```
1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
    14→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    15→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import set_exclusions
    16→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    17→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_default_path
    18→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    19→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    20→from desloppify.state import load_state
    21→
    22→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    25→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    26→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    27→
    28→
    29→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    30→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    31→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    32→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    33→        from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names
    34→
    35→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    36→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    37→
    38→def create_parser():
    39→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    40→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    41→
    42→
    43→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    44→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    45→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    46→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    47→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    48→    if not combined:
    49→        return
    50→    set_exclusions(combined)
    51→    if cli_exclusions:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    54→            file=sys.stderr,
    55→        )
    56→        return
    57→    print(
    58→        colorize(
    59→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    60→        ),
    61→        file=sys.stderr,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    66→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    67→
    68→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    69→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    70→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    71→    """
    72→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    73→        return
    74→    runtime_root = (
    75→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    76→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    77→        else get_project_root()
    78→    )
    79→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    80→        try:
    81→            state_file = state_path(args)
    82→            if state_file:
    83→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    84→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    85→                if saved_path:
    86→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    87→                    return
    88→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    89→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    90→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    91→    if lang:
    92→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
    93→    else:
    94→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
    95→
    96→
    97→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    98→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
    99→    config = load_config()
   100→
   101→    state_file = state_path(args)
   102→    state = load_state(state_file)
   103→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   104→
   105→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   109→    from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
   110→
   111→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   112→
   113→
   114→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   115→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   116→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   117→    try:
   118→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   119→    except SystemExit:
   120→        return
   121→
   122→
   123→def main() -> None:
   124→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   125→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   126→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   127→            try:
   128→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   129→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   130→                logger.debug(
   131→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   132→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   133→                )
   134→
   135→    parser = create_parser()
   136→    args = parser.parse_args()
   137→    if args.command == "help":
   138→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    try:
   142→        with runtime_scope():
   143→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   144→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   145→
   146→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   147→            handler(args)
   148→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   149→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   150→        sys.exit(1)
   151→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   152→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   153→        sys.exit(1)
   154→
   155→
   156→if __name__ == "__main__":
   157→    main()
   158→
```
